Every value is computed from the\ndatabase at call time; no total is embedded.\n\n## Interface\n\n`ledger report monthly` takes no further arguments. Extra arguments are a usage error (exit 2).\n\nOutput on success (CSV, `\\n` endings, no quoting, no trailing spaces):\n\n- Header: `month,transactions,inflow,outflow,net`\n- One row per `YYYY-MM` that has at least one transaction, ascending by month. The month is the\n  first seven characters of the stored date.\n- `transactions` is the count of that month's transactions.\n- `inflow` is the sum of positive amounts, `outflow` the sum of the absolute values of negative\n  amounts (written positive), `net` is `inflow - outflow`.\n- Final row `TOTAL,<count>,<inflow>,<outflow>,<net>` over all transactions.\n- Amounts are computed in integer cents and written with exactly two decimals via `ledger_core.money`.\n  A month with no transactions is absent. An empty ledger prints the header and a TOTAL row of zeros.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Read all transactions with `db.transactions.list(...)`.\n2. Group by the first seven characters of `date` in integer cents; never use floating point.\n3. Emit rows in ascending month order, then the TOTAL row.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-005` — read: list transactions for aggregation - supplies: — - returns: `date`, `amount`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC report-monthly-rows ===\nIntent: Monthly rows, ordering, and the TOTAL row are computed from posted transactions; expected counts and sums are derived from the sum((-a for a in amounts if a < 0), Decimal(0))\n    row = by_month[month]\n    assert int(row[1]) == len(amounts)\n    assert Decimal(row[2]) == inflow\n    assert Decimal(row[3]) == outflow\n    assert Decimal(row[4]) == inflow - outflow\n    for cell in row[2:]:\n        assert len(cell.split(\".\")[1]) == 2\ntotal = table[-1]\nassert total[0] == \"TOTAL\" and len(total) == 5\nassert BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ReportMonthly.md ===\n# FEATURE: Report Monthly\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Implements `ledger report monthly`, the per-month transaction, inflow, outflow, and net totals computed from stored transactions. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CliEntry.md, FEATURE-Validation.md |\n| Provides | ledger report monthly |\n| Consumes | ledger_core.money, cli.registry |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nGive the shop a report of cash movement by calendar month. Every value is computed from the\ndatabase at call time; no total is embedded.\n\n## Interface\n\n`ledger report monthly` takes no further arguments. Extra arguments are a usage error (exit 2).\n\nOutput on success (CSV, `\\n` endings, no quoting, no trailing spaces):\n\n- Header: `month,transactions,inflow,outflow,net`\n- One row per `YYYY-MM` that has at least one transaction, ascending by month. The month is the\n  first seven characters of the stored date.\n- `transactions` is the count of that month's transactions.\n- `inflow` is the sum of positive amounts, `outflow` the sum of the absolute values of negative\n  amounts (written positive), `net` is `inflow - outflow`.\n- Final row `TOTAL,<count>,<inflow>,<outflow>,<net>` over all transactions.\n- Amounts are computed in integer cents and written with exactly two decimals via `ledger_core.money`.\n  A month with no transactions is absent. An empty ledger prints the header and a TOTAL row of zeros.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Read all transactions with `db.transactions.list(...)`.\n2. Group by the first seven characters of `date` in integer cents; never use floating point.\n3. Emit rows in ascending month order, then the TOTAL row.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TXN-005` — read: list transactions for aggregation - supplies: — - returns: `date`, `amount`\n\n## FEATURE-NoNegativeBalance.md ===\n# FEATURE: No Negative Balance\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Assembly story verifying that load, post, amend, delete, and transfer each reject any command that would leave an account balance below zero and change nothing. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-LoadTransactions.md, FEATURE-TxnPost.md, FEATURE-TxnAmend.md, FEATURE-TxnDelete.md, FEATURE-Transfer.md |\n| Provides | — |\n| Consumes | ledger load transactions, ledger txn post, ledger txn amend, ledger txn delete, ledger transfer |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAssembly and intent only. An account's balance is the sum of its transaction amounts and must never\nbe below zero. For each of load, post, amend, delete, and transfer, a case that would leave a\nbalance below zero exits 3 and leaves balances and row counts unchanged. A load file whose later\nrows make an earlier-valid balance negative is rejected whole.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC nonneg-load ===\nIntent: A transactions load whose earlier rows are individually valid but whose combined effect makes the balance negative is rejected whole with exit 3 and stores none of its rows.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ndef parse(out):\n    return list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n\ndef counts():\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"count\")\n    assert code == 0\n    return {r[0]: int(r[1]) for r in parse(out)[1:]}\n\nacct = \"ZZNL1\"\ncat = \"ZZNLC\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    if db.categories.get(category=cat) is None:\n        db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Load Probe\")\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(\"account\", \"add\", acct, \"Load Probe\")\nassert code == 0\nbefore = counts()\nrows = [\n    [\"800000001\", \"2099-03-01\", acct, cat, \"50.00\", \"in\"],\n    [\"800000002\", \"2099-03-02\", acct, cat, \"-30.00\", \"out one\"],\n    [\"800000003\", \"2099-03-03\", acct, cat, \"-30.00\", \"out two\"],\n]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"txns.csv\")\n    with w.writerows(rows)\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", \"transactions\", path)\nprint(out, err)\nassert code == 3\nassert out == \"\"\nassert counts() == before\ncode, out, err = | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Terminal gate that runs the supplied end-to-end grader and the project's own test suite. |\n| Depends On | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CommandContract.md, FEATURE-NoNegativeBalance.md |\n| Provides | — |\n| Consumes | ledger |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nTerminal story. After every other story has closed, run `sh sources/run.sh` and `sh bin/test.sh`,\neach of which must exit zero. Nothing under `sources/` is modified. Failures are fixed in the\nowning module, never by editing the grader or its seed files.\n\nThe grader creates its own temporary SQLite database and runs `ledger init`, which empties the\ndatabase. The criterion therefore removes `DATABASE_URL` and `DATABASE_PATH` from the otherwise\ninherited environment so the managed store is never touched by a child process.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC verification-full-suite ===\nIntent: The supplied grader and the project's own test suite both exit zero when run from the application root against their own temporary databases.\nSuite: full\nRequires: executable=sh;
